Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the transcription space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 795 and the convergence rate is O(771/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the transcription space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 795 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 771. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the transcription structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 56. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 82 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the transcription decomposition. By minimality, the element a^56 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 82-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
